# Repository Atlas — Phase 2 (300+ discovery target)

**AK · generated 2026-11-02 from live GitHub API payloads** — every stars/pushed/license/homepage/topics value below was returned by `api.github.com` on 2026-11-01/02; nothing in this table is from memory or inference.

## 0. How this atlas was built (method, not vibes)

| Step | Detail |
|---|---|
| Discovery calls | 2926 raw (repo, query) records from 3 methods |
| Method 1 — topics | 50 GitHub **topic listings** (`/topics/{t}/repositories`, 2 pages each) → curatorially organised by GitHub, not by my keywords |
| Method 2 — maintainer orgs | 18 org/user listings (`/orgs/{org}/repos?sort=updated`) for pmndrs, greensock, darkroomengineering, motiondivision, framer, oframe, mrdoob, Tympanus, shuding, vercel-labs, cvdlab, patriciogonzalezvivo, drcmda, wagerfield, ktsianos, yvesbezy, OmarIsmail, codybontecou |
| Method 3 — search | ~110 **query shapes** per category (`topic:…`, `stars:>N`, `pushed:>date`, technique keywords), 30 results each, paced to the 30/min search limit |
| Deduplication | by `full_name`; metadata kept from the richest record → **2566 unique repositories** |
| Relevance filter | rule-based category match on name + description + topics + the query that found it → **1914 in-scope** |
| Quality bar | composite score = √stars (sub-linear, so mega-repos don't drown small gems) + recency + licence + demo/homepage + topic richness + framework fit − archived penalty → **882 above the deep bar (≥25)**, 1107 above the atlas bar (≥18) |
| Star-agnostic by design | topics + org listings + `pushed:>date` queries surfaced maintained small repos; 100k★ and 150★ repos are scored on the same curve |

**Evaluation statuses used below** — `· discovered` (metadata verified only) → `▲ shortlisted` (score ≥ 25, read the listing + description + topics) → `◆ inspected (deep)` (README/source read, card in `TOP_35_DEEP_EVALUATIONS.md`) → `★ recommended` (in `TOP_15_AC_REFERENCES.md`). No repo is claimed as tested unless `frontend-intelligence/prototype-lab/` says so.

## 1. Coverage against the discovery targets

| Category | Target | In-scope | Atlas-quality (≥18) | Deep (≥25) | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Three.js / React Three Fiber / Drei | 45 | **161** | 100 | 87 | ✅ met |
| GSAP / ScrollTrigger / scroll experiences | 40 | **151** | 75 | 52 | ✅ met |
| Motion / Framer Motion / UI animation | 30 | **138** | 79 | 68 | ✅ met |
| WebGL / GLSL / shader techniques | 35 | **266** | 190 | 157 | ✅ met |
| Premium website implementations | 40 | **235** | 136 | 104 | ✅ met |
| Creative frontend components | 35 | **152** | 88 | 70 | ✅ met |
| Typography / visual design systems | 20 | **192** | 115 | 89 | ✅ met |
| Performance / accessibility | 25 | **160** | 97 | 80 | ✅ met |
| Document discovery / search UX | 30 | **459** | 227 | 175 | ✅ met |
| **TOTAL** | **300** | **1914** | **1107** | **882** | unique repos catalogued: **2566** |

Repos seen but filed out of primary scope (games engines, CLI tools, unrelated `react`/`animation` hits): 652. They are deliberately excluded from the counts above rather than used as padding.

## 2. Licence reality (the number AO actually needs)

| SPDX | repos | meaning for us |
|---|---|---|
| `MIT` | 977 | safe to use; attribution in source headers |
| `NONE` | 539 | **no licence file** → all rights reserved; read-only reference, do not copy or depend on |
| `NOASSERTION` | 144 | custom/unclear — legal review before any copying |
| `Apache-2.0` | 99 | safe; patent grant; NOTICE if redistributing |
| `GPL-3.0` | 36 | copyleft — depend on it, never vendor its code |
| `OFL-1.1` | 32 | SIL Open Font — fonts only, no derivative naming |
| `AGPL-3.0` | 20 | network copyleft — do **not** vendor; idea-level reference only |
| `BSD-3-Clause` | 12 | safe |
| `ISC` | 9 | safe |
| `Unlicense` | 7 | public domain |
| `CC0-1.0` | 6 | public domain (art assets too) |
| `CC-BY-4.0` | 6 | attribution required; usually assets, not code |
| `BSD-2-Clause` | 4 | safe |
| `MPL-2.0` | 4 | file-level copyleft — fine to depend on |

No-licence or custom-licence in-scope repos: **683** of 1914 — all of them are usable as *reading material*, and every one is flagged in the tables below (`⚠️`) so AC never mistakes one for vendorable code.

## 3. The atlas

Columns: **Repo** · **★** · **pushed** · **maint.** (active ≤120 d · maturing ≤365 d · slow ≤730 d · stale >730 d · ARCHIVED) · **lic** · **tech** (API `language` + `topics`) · **demo** (`homepage` field) · **what it demonstrates** (API `description`, verbatim) · **status**.

## 4. Long tail (in-scope but below the atlas quality bar)

## 5. Reproducibility

Discovery + scoring scripts live in `/home/user/research/` (`discover.py`, `catalog.py`, `gen_atlas.py`, `deep_eval.py`); the machine-readable full catalogue is `frontend-intelligence/_data/repository-catalog.json` (one object per repo: `repo,url,stars,forks,pushed,created,language,license,license_note,archived,homepage,topics,size_kb,maintenance,react_next,categories,primary,found_via,score,status`). Re-running `discover.py` resumes from existing records and only issues new API calls.
